<a id='question-050'></a>

### Question 050: Behavioral Modification and Operant Conditioning
- **Difficulty**: Medium
- **Core Concept / 重点考点**: Understanding the principles of operant conditioning, specifically positive reinforcement, negative reinforcement, positive punishment, and negative punishment, and their application in modifying behavior. (理解行为矫正原则，特别是正强化、负强化、正惩罚和负惩罚，以及它们在行为矫正中的应用。)

#### Clinical Vignette:
A 6-year-old boy, Alex, consistently refuses to clean his room when asked by his parents. His mother, frustrated, tells him that if he cleans his room before dinner, he can watch his favorite cartoon show afterward. Alex, who enjoys watching cartoons, cleans his room promptly. His father, observing this, decides to implement a new strategy. He tells Alex that if Alex does *not* clean his room, he will be grounded from playing video games for the rest of the week. Alex, who dislikes being grounded, cleans his room.

Which of the following best describes the behavioral modification strategy used by Alex's mother?

(A) Negative punishment
(B) Positive punishment
(C) Negative reinforcement
(D) Positive reinforcement
(E) Extinction

#### Correct Answer: (D)

#### High-Yield Mechanism & Behavioral/Statistical Explanation:
##### 1. Clinical Vignette Breakdown & Key Clues (题干与题眼拆解)
- **Alex's initial behavior:** Refuses to clean his room.
- **Mother's intervention:** States that cleaning the room (behavior) will lead to watching a cartoon (desirable stimulus).
- **Alex's subsequent behavior:** Cleans his room.
- **Father's intervention:** States that *not* cleaning the room (behavior) will lead to being grounded (aversive stimulus).
- **Alex's subsequent behavior:** Cleans his room.
- **Question focus:** Identify the type of operant conditioning used by the *mother*.

The mother's strategy involves adding a desirable stimulus (watching cartoons) *after* the desired behavior (cleaning the room) occurs, thereby increasing the likelihood of the behavior occurring again. This is the definition of positive reinforcement.

##### 2. Conceptual, Neurobiological & Ethical Deep-Dive (行为医学、脑机制、统计原理与医学伦理深度剖析)
- **Operant Conditioning (Skinner):** Learning through consequences. Behaviors are strengthened if followed by reinforcement and weakened if followed by punishment.
- **Reinforcement:** Increases the likelihood of a behavior.
    - **Positive Reinforcement:** Adding a desirable stimulus after a behavior (e.g., giving a treat for sitting). (增加一个愉悦刺激以增强行为)
    - **Negative Reinforcement:** Removing an aversive stimulus after a behavior (e.g., turning off an alarm clock by pressing snooze). (移除一个不愉悦刺激以增强行为)
- **Punishment:** Decreases the likelihood of a behavior.
    - **Positive Punishment:** Adding an aversive stimulus after a behavior (e.g., spanking a child). (增加一个不愉悦刺激以减少行为)
    - **Negative Punishment:** Removing a desirable stimulus after a behavior (e.g., taking away a toy). (移除一个愉悦刺激以减少行为)
- **Extinction:** Weakening of a conditioned response when the reinforcement is withheld (e.g., ignoring a child's tantrum). (停止强化以减少行为)

In the vignette, the mother *adds* the desirable stimulus (cartoon) *after* Alex performs the desired behavior (cleaning). This fits the definition of positive reinforcement. The father uses positive punishment (adding grounding) to decrease the behavior of not cleaning.

#### Distractor Analysis (全干扰项逐一深度纠错剖析):
- (A) **Negative punishment:** This involves *removing* a desirable stimulus to decrease a behavior. For example, taking away Alex's video games because he didn't clean his room. This is what the father did, not the mother.
- (B) **Positive punishment:** This involves *adding* an aversive stimulus to decrease a behavior. For example, spanking Alex for not cleaning his room. Neither parent used this strategy.
- (C) **Negative reinforcement:** This involves *removing* an aversive stimulus to increase a behavior. For example, if Alex cleaned his room to stop his mother from nagging him. This was not described in the mother's strategy.
- (E) **Extinction:** This involves withholding reinforcement for a previously reinforced behavior, leading to a decrease in that behavior. For example, if the parents completely ignored Alex's refusal to clean his room without providing any consequence (positive or negative reinforcement/punishment). This was not the strategy used.

#### High-Yield Comparison Table (核心考点与易混淆概念横向对比):
| Concept             | Definition                                                                 | Example (Alex)                                                                 | Vignette Application |
|----------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------|----------------------|
| Positive Reinforcement | Adding a desirable stimulus to increase behavior.                          | Adding cartoon time after cleaning room.                                          | **Mother's Strategy** |
| Negative Reinforcement  | Removing an aversive stimulus to increase behavior.                         | Cleaning room to stop nagging (not described).                                    | Incorrect            |
| Positive Punishment    | Adding an aversive stimulus to decrease behavior.                          | Spanking for not cleaning (not described).                                        | Incorrect            |
| Negative Punishment    | Removing a desirable stimulus to decrease behavior.                         | Taking away video games for not cleaning (Father's Strategy).                      | Incorrect            |
| Extinction            | Withholding reinforcement to decrease behavior.                             | Ignoring refusal to clean (not described).                                        | Incorrect            |

#### Educational Objective (USMLE High-Yield Takeaway & Differentials):
The core principle is differentiating between reinforcement (increasing behavior) and punishment (decreasing behavior), and further distinguishing between positive (adding a stimulus) and negative (removing a stimulus) forms. The mother used positive reinforcement by adding a desirable stimulus (cartoon) after the desired behavior (cleaning). Key differentials include confusing positive reinforcement with negative reinforcement (removing something unpleasant to increase behavior) or positive punishment (adding something unpleasant to decrease behavior).

---
